can be uniquely recovered through the identity
g(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rm i}\xi x}\widehat{g}(\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi
, for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
.
Note in particular that Fubini implies

\displaystyle g^{\prime\prime}(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\partial_{xx}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rm i}\xi x}\widehat{g}(\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{xx}\left(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rm i}\xi x}\widehat{g}(\xi)\right)\mathrm{d}\xi

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\xi^{2}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rm i}\xi x}\widehat{g}(\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi.

We have, for all
r\leq t
,

\displaystyle G_{r}(t)=\mathbb{E}_{r}[g(V_{t})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{E}_{r}\Big[\mathrm{e}^{-{\rm i}\xi V_{t}}\Big]\widehat{g}(\xi)\mathrm{d}\xi

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\widehat{g}(\xi)\exp\Big\{-{\rm i}\xi\Theta^{r}_{t}-\frac{1}{4H}\xi^{2}(t-r)^{2H}\Big\}\mathrm{d}\xi.

Noting that
g^{\prime}(x)=-\frac{{\rm i}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\widehat{g}(\xi)\xi\mathrm{e}^{{\rm i}\xi x}\mathrm{d}\xi
and
g^{\prime\prime}(x)=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\widehat{g}(\xi)\xi^{2}\mathrm{e}^{{\rm i}\xi x}\mathrm{d}\xi
, we obtain

\displaystyle G_{r}^{\prime}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\widehat{g}(\xi)\left(-{\rm i}\xi\partial_{t}\Theta^{r}_{t}-\frac{1}{2}\xi^{2}(t-r)^{2H-1}\right)\mathrm{e}^{{\rm i}\xi\Theta^{r}_{t}-\frac{\xi^{2}}{4H}(t-r)^{2H}}\mathrm{d}\xi

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{g}(\xi)\left(-{\rm i}\xi\partial_{t}\Theta^{r}_{t}-\frac{1}{2}\xi^{2}(t-r)^{2H-1}\right)\mathbb{E}_{r}[\mathrm{e}^{{\rm i}\xi V_{t}}]\mathrm{d}\xi

(4.3)

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{t}\Theta^{r}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{r}[g^{\prime}(V_{t})]+\frac{(t-r)^{2H-1}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{r}[g^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\right).

Since we have
\partial_{s}\Theta^{r}_{s}=(H-\frac{1}{2})\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{u}
, this entails

\displaystyle G_{r}(t)-G_{r}(t_{i})

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{i}}^{t}G^{\prime}_{r}(s)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{r}[g^{\prime}(V_{t})]\left(H-\frac{1}{2}\right)\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{u}+\frac{(s-r)^{2H-1}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{r}[g^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\right\}\mathrm{d}s.

The result is finally extended from Schwartz function to
\mathcal{C}^{2}
as in the proof of
[
14
, Lemma 2.2]
, simply by applying the conditional version of the dominated convergence theorem in place of the standard result.
∎

4.2.
Fractional calculus

Recall that
t_{i}\leq t\leq t_{i+1}
.
For
\alpha\in(-\frac{1}{2},2H]
, define

(4.4)

\diamondsuit_{\alpha}(\Delta):=\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H<\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H>\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta|\log(\Delta)|\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H=\frac{1}{2}},

and in particular

(4.5)

\bigstar(\Delta):=\diamondsuit_{2H}(\Delta)=\Delta^{3H+\frac{1}{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{H<\frac{1}{6}}+\Delta\mathbbm{1}_{H>\frac{1}{6}}+\Delta|\log(\Delta)|\mathbbm{1}_{H=\frac{1}{6}}.

Lemma 4.3
.

For all
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
,
r\in[t_{j-1},t_{j})
,
j<i
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert(t-r)^{\alpha}-(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}\right\rvert\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha}(i-j)^{\alpha-1}
. In particular, this entails

\displaystyle\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\lesssim\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}
.

Lemma 4.4
.

For
\alpha\in(-\frac{1}{2},2H]
,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\big((t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{d}r\right|\lesssim\diamondsuit_{\alpha}(\Delta).

Lemma 4.5
.

For
j<i
,
\alpha\in(-\frac{1}{2},0]
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}\left\lvert K(t_{i},r)-K(t_{j},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim(t_{i}-t_{j})^{H+\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}
.

Lemma 4.6
.

For
t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

The following is a mild modification of
[
17
, Lemma 2.1 (2.2)]
which features the logarithmic correction for
H=\frac{1}{6}
that the author refers to in the published version of the paper.

Lemma 4.7
.

For
t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(t-r)^{2H}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Lemma 4.8
.

\displaystyle S:=\Delta^{2H}\sum_{j=1}^{i-2}|i-j-1|^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-1}|i-\ell-1|^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim~\bigstar(\Delta)
.

4.3.
Proofs of the lemmas

Proof of Lemma
4.3
.

Let
r\in[t_{j-1},t_{j})
and
j\leq i-1
; the lemma follows from

\left\lvert(t-r)^{\alpha}-(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}\right\rvert=\left|\alpha\right|\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{\alpha-1}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim(t_{i}-t_{j})^{\alpha-1}(t-t_{i})\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha}(i-j)^{\alpha-1}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
4.4
.

First we decompose the integral

(4.6)

\int_{0}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\big[(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\big]\mathrm{d}r=\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-1}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{i},r)\big[(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\big]\mathrm{d}r,

for which we check that

\displaystyle\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\big((t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{H-\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}+\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{t_{i-2}}^{t_{i-1}}K(t_{i},r)\big((t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\int_{t_{i-2}}^{t_{i-1}}(t_{i}-r)^{H-\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}.

Then we recall that for
r\in[t_{\ell},t_{\ell+1})
and
\ell\leq i-2
, Lemma
4.3
yields

\left\lvert(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\right\rvert\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha}(i-\ell-1)^{\alpha-1}.

Combined with
K(t_{i},r)\leq K(t_{i},t_{\ell+1})=\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}
, it entails

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{i},r)\big((t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}-(t-r)^{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}\lesssim\diamondsuit_{\alpha}(\Delta).

For the last inequality, we used the fact that
i-1\leq N=\Delta^{-1}
and that

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=2}^{i-1}\ell^{H-\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}\int_{\ell-1}^{\ell}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=2}^{i-1}\int_{\ell-1}^{\ell}s^{H-\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}\mathrm{d}s

(4.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim\log(i-1)\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H=\frac{1}{2}}+(i-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}+\alpha}\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H>\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H<\frac{1}{2}}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
4.5
.

We split the integral into three parts that we study separately:

(4.8)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}\left\lvert K(t_{i},r)-K(t_{j},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r

(4.9)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}(K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r))\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}(K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r))\mathrm{d}r+\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=:I_{[0,j-1]}+I_{[j-1,j]}+I_{[j,i]}.

Regarding
I_{[j,i]}
, an explicit integration yields the upper bound

\displaystyle I_{[j,i]}=\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha+H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}}{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}

(4.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}.

For
I_{[j-1,j]}
, we proceed bounding separately the two components in it, obtaining

(4.11)

\displaystyle I_{[j-1,j]}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}r-\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha+H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-t_{j})^{\alpha}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim(i-j)^{\alpha}\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim(i-j)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}.

Without loss of generality we now assume
j\geq 2
.
Note that
I_{[0,j-1]}
can be bounded above as

(4.12)

I_{[0,j-1]}\leq(t_{i}-t_{j-1})^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}(K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r))\mathrm{d}r=:\Delta^{\alpha}(i-(j-1))^{\alpha}\widetilde{I}_{[0,j-1]},

with
\widetilde{I}_{[0,j-1]}:=\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}(K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r))\mathrm{d}r=\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-2}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}(K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r))\mathrm{d}r
.
For each term in the sum,
r\in[t_{\ell},t_{\ell+1})
, we proceed as in the proof of Lemma
4.4
and obtain the following upper bound (independent of
r
):

\displaystyle K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r)\leq K(t_{j},t_{\ell+1})-K(t_{i},t_{\ell})=\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\left((j-(\ell+1))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-(i-\ell)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\right),

as
K(t,\cdot)
is increasing for
H-\frac{1}{2}<0
, so that

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{[0,j-1]}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-2}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}(K(t_{j},r)-K(t_{i},r))\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-2}\left((j-(\ell+1))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-(i-\ell)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-2}\left((j-(\ell+1))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-(i-\ell)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{j-1}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-\sum_{k=i-(j-2)}^{i}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

If
j-1\leq i-(j-1)
, then, ignoring the positive second term, we write, using (
4.1
)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{[0,j-1]}\leq\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{j-1}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\leq\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{i-(j-1)}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{i-(j-1)}x^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}.

Now, if
j-1>i-(j-1)
, then the two sums overlap, so that, using (
4.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{[0,j-1]}

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{i-(j-2)}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-\sum_{k=j-1}^{i-1}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{i-(j-2)}k^{H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{i-(j-2)}x^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}x\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining these two cases with (
4.12
), we thus obtain

(4.13)

\displaystyle I_{[0,j-1]}\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-(j-1))^{\alpha}(i-j)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}.

The lemma follows from plugging the upper bounds in (
4.3
)-(
4.11
)-(
4.13
) into (
4.8
)

\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{\alpha}\left\lvert K(t_{i},r)-K(t_{j},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
4.6
.

For
r\in[t_{j},t_{j+1})
,
t_{i}-\kappa_{r}=t_{i}-t_{j}=\Delta(i-j)
.
By Lemma
4.3
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}(i-j)^{2H}\Delta^{2H}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{i-2}(i-j)^{2H}\Delta^{3H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta^{2H}_{t}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r.

The last integral is dominated by
\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}
. Furthermore, the simple observation

(i-j)^{2H}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}=\left(\frac{i-j}{i-j-1}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-H}(i-j)^{3H-\frac{3}{2}}\leq 2^{\frac{3}{2}-H}(i-j)^{3H-\frac{3}{2}}.

allows us to compute, similarly to (
4.3
) with
\alpha=2H
,

(4.14)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{i-2}(i-j)^{2H}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{j=0}^{i-2}(i-j)^{3H-\frac{3}{2}}

(4.15)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{i^{3H-\frac{1}{2}}-1}{3H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{H\neq\frac{1}{6}}+\log(i)\mathbbm{1}_{H=\frac{1}{6}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}-3H}\mathbbm{1}_{H>\frac{1}{6}}+\mathbbm{1}_{H<\frac{1}{6}}+|\log(\Delta)|\mathbbm{1}_{H=\frac{1}{6}}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
4.7
.

Similarly to the previous proof, noting that for any
r\in[t_{j},t_{j+1}]
with
j\leq i-2
, we have
(t-r)^{2H}\leq(t_{i+1}-t_{j})^{2H}\leq(2(t_{i}-t_{j}))^{2H}
, it yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(t-r)^{2H}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{3H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{i-2}(i-j)^{3H-\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{2H}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t}\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r.

The the claim follows from (
4.14
).
∎

Proof of Lemma
4.8
.

For
\ell=j-1
then
\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}r=\frac{1}{H+\frac{1}{2}}\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}
and similarly to (
4.14
),
\sum_{j=1}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\lesssim 1
.
If
\ell<j-1
, then
\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}r\leq(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}
.
We plug this in the rest of the sum in Lemma
4.8
, we dispatch the
H-\frac{3}{2}
exponent in the following way and exploit
(i-\ell-1)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\leq(i-j-1)^{-\frac{1}{2}}
:

\displaystyle S^{\prime}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=1}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-2}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-2}(i-\ell-1)^{H-1}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-4}(i-\ell-1)^{H-1}\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-1}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}},

where we swapped the sums in the last line.
Furthermore,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-1}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}\int_{j}^{j+1}(i-s-1)^{H-1}(s-\ell-2)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\int_{\ell+2}^{i-1}(i-s-1)^{H-1}(s-(\ell+2))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=(i-\ell-1)^{H-1}(i-\ell-3)^{2H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{B}\left(H,H+\frac{1}{2}\right),

since
\int_{0}^{x}(x-y)^{a-1}y^{b-1}\mathrm{d}y=x^{a+b-1}\mathrm{B}(a,b)
for
a,b>-1
, and with
\mathrm{B}
the Beta function.
Moreover, since
(i-\ell-1)^{H-1}\leq(i-\ell-3)^{H-1}
, similarly to (
4.3
) we obtain

\displaystyle S^{\prime}\lesssim\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-4}(i-\ell-3)^{3H-\frac{3}{2}}=1+\int_{1}^{i-3}s^{3H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle 1,&\text{if }H<\frac{1}{6},\\
\displaystyle\log(i-3),&\text{if }H=\frac{1}{6},\\
\displaystyle(i-3)^{3H-\frac{1}{2}},&\text{if }H>\frac{1}{6}.\end{array}\right.

Note that
i-3\leq N=1/\Delta
and
S\lesssim\Delta^{2H}\left(\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}S^{\prime}+\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\right)
, this concludes the proof.
∎

5.
Proof of Proposition
3.10

We fix
i\leq N
and
t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})
and recall that
H\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.
To express the differences in
\mathfrak{B}_{1}(t)
and
\mathfrak{B}_{2}(t)
without invoking the strong rate of convergence (that is without absolute values), we rely on a combination of the Clark-Ocone and the integration by parts formulae that allows us to decouple the stochastic terms from the kernels and saves us from the immediate use of crude estimates such as the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. For some of the terms in the following computations, several applications of this strategy are necessary.
Let us begin with the Clark-Ocone formula:

\displaystyle\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}\Big]+\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{D}^{W}_{r}\mathbb{E}_{r}\Big[\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}\Big]\mathrm{d}W_{r}.

Note
\mathcal{U}_{r}:=\mathrm{D}^{W}_{r}(\partial_{x}^{2}\bar{u}_{t})
and
\varphi_{r}(t)=\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime}(V_{t})]
.
Integration by parts then yields

(5.1)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}_{1}(t)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{x}^{2}\bar{u}_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}\Big[\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{U}_{r}\mathbb{E}_{r}\Big[(\psi^{2})^{\prime}(V_{t})K(t,r)-(\psi^{2})^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})K(t_{i},r)\Big]\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{x}^{2}\bar{u}_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}\Big[\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{U}_{r}\varphi_{r}(t)\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{U}_{r}\big(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{r}(t_{i})\big)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\right]

(5.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{x}^{2}\bar{u}_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}\Big[\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}\Big]

(5.3)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{U}_{t}\varphi_{t}(t)\right]\int_{0}^{t}\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r

(5.4)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{U}_{t}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{t}(t)\Big)\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

(5.5)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\varphi_{r}(t)\Big(\mathcal{U}_{r}-\mathcal{U}_{t}\Big)\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

(5.6)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathcal{U}_{r}\big(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{r}(t_{i})\big)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\right]

(5.7)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{k=1}^{5}\bm{\Xi}_{k}

For future reference, we first compute, for all
r\in[0,t]
,

(5.8)

\mathcal{U}_{r}=\mathrm{D}^{W}_{r}(\partial_{x}^{2}\bar{u}_{t})=\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left\{\int_{r}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K({\kappa_{s}},r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right\}\right].

The first two terms
\bm{\Xi}_{1},\bm{\Xi}_{2}
are immediate and yield rate one by Lemma
3.8
and the next comment and lemma.
Indeed, the growth of
\phi,\psi
and their derivatives imply that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\phi^{(3)}\left(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}\right)\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)\right\rvert^{p}\right]

is uniformly bounded across
r\leq t\leq T
for all
p>1
.

Lemma 5.1
.

\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{1}\right\rvert+\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{2}\right\rvert\lesssim t^{2H}-t_{i}^{2H}
.

Proof.

Thanks to the growth of
\phi,\psi
and their derivatives, the estimates of
X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}
and
V
in (
2.27
), the square integrability of
K
over
[t,T]
, we can conclude

(5.9)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{1}\right\rvert\lesssim\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\psi(V_{t})^{2}-\psi(V_{t_{i}})^{2}\right]\right\rvert\lesssim t^{2H}-t_{i}^{2H},

(5.10)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{2}\right\rvert\lesssim\left\lvert\int_{0}^{t}\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right\rvert\lesssim t^{2H}-t_{i}^{2H}.\qed

The other three terms require some tedious, but unambiguous, computations that are developed in the following sections.

5.1.
Bound for
\Xi_{3}

Recall that
\bm{\Xi}_{3}
was defined in (
5.7
).

Proposition 5.2
.

\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{3}\right\rvert\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Proof.

We apply the Clark-Ocone formula with respect to the Brownian motion
W
:

(5.11)

\varphi_{t}(t)-\varphi_{r}(t)=\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{q}[\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}\varphi_{q}(t)]\mathrm{d}W_{q}=\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]K(t,q)\mathrm{d}W_{q}.

Using integration by parts, this implies

(5.12)

\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{t}(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{t}(t))\Big]=-\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{r}^{t}\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}\mathcal{U}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]K(t,q)\right]\mathrm{d}q.

Looking into the integrand, we compute

\displaystyle\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}\mathcal{U}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\psi(V_{t_{i}})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{q}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}^{t}})K(\kappa_{s}^{t},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right)\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\psi(V_{t_{i}})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})K(t_{i},q)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\int_{q}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\psi(V_{t_{i}})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right)\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\psi(V_{t_{i}})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right]+\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})K(t_{i},q)\right]

(5.13)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{m=1}^{4}\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(m)}.

We thus have

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{U}_{t}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{t}(t)\Big)\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathrm{D}_{q}\mathcal{U}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\Big]K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q\right)\Big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=-\sum_{m=1}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(m)}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\right]K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q\right)\Big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big)\mathrm{d}r=:-\sum_{m=1}^{4}\bm{\Xi}_{3m}.

We proceed by bounding each of these terms.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{31})}
.

By Lemma
4.1
,

\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(1)}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\Big]\right\rvert\lesssim\Big(1+K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta}\Big)\mathbbm{1}_{q<t_{i}}
.
Then, for
q<t_{i}
,

\displaystyle\int_{r}^{t}K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta}K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta}\left[\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i-1}\int_{t_{j-1}\vee r}^{t_{j}}K(t_{j},q)K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q+\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t,q)K(t_{i},q)\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\Delta}\left(\sum_{j=\ell}^{i-1}K(t,t_{j})\int_{t_{j-1}\vee r}^{t_{j}}K(t_{j},q)\mathrm{d}q+\Delta^{2H}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\Delta}\left(\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=\ell}^{i-1}\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-j)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}+2\Delta^{2H}\right),

since
K(t,t_{j})\leq K(t_{i},t_{j})=\big(\Delta(i-j)\big)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}
.
Thus, using
t_{\ell-1}\leq r<t_{\ell}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\ell}^{i-1}(i-j)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}=\sum_{j=1}^{i-\ell}j^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\leq\int_{0}^{i-\ell}s^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim(i-\ell)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{t_{i}-t_{\ell}}{\Delta}\right)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}

(5.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{t_{i}-r}{\Delta}\right)^{H+\frac{1}{2}},

and hence

(5.15)

\displaystyle\int_{r}^{t}K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta}K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q\lesssim\Delta^{H}(t_{i}-r)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{2H+\frac{1}{2}}.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{32})}
.

We note that
\mathcal{U}_{t}^{(2)}
is bounded in
L^{2}(\Omega)
, hence
\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(2)}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\Big]\lesssim 1
.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{33})}
.

By Cauchy-Schwarz’ and Jensen’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(3)}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right]\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})^{2}(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)K(s,t)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t}^{T}K(s,q)^{2}K(s,t)^{2}\mathrm{d}s\leq K(t,q)^{2}\frac{(T-t)^{2H}}{2H}\lesssim K(t,q)^{2}.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{34})}
.

Similarly we write
\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(4)}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\Big]^{2}\lesssim\mathbb{E}\big[\lvert\mathcal{U}_{t,q}^{(4)}\lvert^{2}\big]\lesssim K(t_{i},q)^{2}
.

We then regroup all those estimates together.
By (
5.12
)-(
5.1
),
the upper bound (
5.15
) and the above estimates, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{t}(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{t}(t))\Big]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{4}\mathcal{U}^{(m)}_{t,q}\Big)\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\Big]K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{r}^{t}\left(1+K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta}+K(t,q)+K(t_{i},q)\right)K(t,q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim(t-r)^{2H}+(t_{i}-r)^{2H}\mathbbm{1}_{r<t_{i}}.

To conclude, we notice

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{2H}\Big|K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big|\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t-r)^{2H}\Big|K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big|\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}(t-r)^{2H}\Big|K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big|\mathrm{d}r,

such that we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{3}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}(t-r)^{2H}\Big|K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big|\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{t_{i}}(t_{i}-r)^{2H}\Big|K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big|\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{0}^{t}(t-r)^{2H}\Big|K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\Big|\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta),

using Lemma
4.7
, which concludes the proof.
∎

5.2.
Bound for
\Xi_{4}

Recall the definition of
\bm{\Xi}_{4}
from (
5.7
).

Proposition 5.3
.

|\bm{\Xi}_{4}|\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta).

Proof.

We recall the expression of
\mathcal{U}_{r}
given in (
5.8
), which yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{r}-\mathcal{U}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left\{\int_{r}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K({\kappa_{s}},r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})\big(K(s,r)-K(s,t)\big)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho[\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})]\right\}\right].

This expression requires integration by parts because Cauchy-Schwarz yields a suboptimal rate.
We divide and conquer in the following way:

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{4}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\varphi_{r}(t)\Big(\mathcal{U}_{r}-\mathcal{U}_{t}\Big)\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\varphi_{r}(t)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{r}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K({\kappa_{s}},r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right)\right]\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\varphi_{r}(t)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})\big(K(s,r)-K(s,t)\big)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right)\right]\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t}\rho\varphi_{r}(t)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\Big(\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})\Big)\right]\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right]

\displaystyle=:\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{\Xi}_{41}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{42}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{43}(r)\Big]\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r.

The bound then follows from
Propositions
5.4
-
5.7
-
5.8
, noting that

\Delta^{2H+1}\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.
∎

In spirit, the proofs show that

\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\varphi_{r}(t)(\mathcal{U}_{r}-\mathcal{U}_{t})]\right\rvert\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}\mathbbm{1}_{r<t_{i}}+(t-r)^{2H},

which is sufficient to conclude by Lemmas
4.6
and
4.7
.

5.2.1.
Bound for
\Xi_{41}

Proposition 5.4
.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Xi}_{41}(r)\right]\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right\rvert\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Proof.

Applying integration by parts, we obtain the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Xi}_{41}(r)]\right\rvert

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{r}^{t}\Big(\mathrm{D}^{B}_{s}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})+\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}^{B}_{s}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\Big)\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\lvert\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})\right]K(t,s)K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s\right\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lvert\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{s}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})K(\kappa_{q},s)\mathrm{d}B_{q}\right)\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})\right]K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s\right\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lvert\int_{r}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{q})K(q,s)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\psi(V_{\kappa_{s}})\right)\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})\right]K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{r}^{t}K(t,s)K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s+\int_{r}^{t}K(\kappa_{s}+\Delta,s)\sqrt{\Delta}K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s+\int_{r}^{t}K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s,

where we used Lemma
4.1
.
Since
1\lesssim K(t,s)
for all
s>0
,
we write

\displaystyle\left\lvert\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Xi}_{41}(r)\right]\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\left[\int_{r}^{t}K(t,s)K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s+\int_{r}^{t}K(\kappa_{s}+\Delta,s)\sqrt{\Delta}K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s\right]\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{411}+\bm{\Xi}_{412}.

The proof then follows from
Lemma
5.5
and Lemma
5.6
below,
since
\Delta\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.
∎

Lemma 5.5
.

\bm{\Xi}_{411}\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Lemma 5.6
.

\bm{\Xi}_{412}\lesssim\Delta
.

Proof of Lemma
5.5
.

If
r\in[t_{i},t)
then
\int_{r}^{t}K(t,s)K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s=0
.
For
r\in[0,t_{i})
,
s\in[t_{j},t_{j+1})
with
j\leq i-2
, it holds
K(t,s)\leq(t_{i}-t_{j+1})^{H-\frac{1}{2}}=\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}
; thus

(5.16)

\displaystyle\int_{r}^{t}K(t,s)K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i-2}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t,s)K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}s+\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t,s)K(t_{i-1},r)\mathrm{d}s+\int_{t_{i}}^{t}K(t,s)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i-2}K(t_{j},r)(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\Big(K(t_{i-1},r)+K(t_{i},r)\Big)

(5.17)

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{4111}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{4112}(r).

For the last term on the right-hand side of the equation above, note that, for
j=i
,

\int_{0}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{2H}

by Lemma
4.4
(and
similarly
j=i-1
).
Thus

\int_{0}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{4112}(r)\big(K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{3H+\frac{1}{2}}
.
Now, we assume
i\geq 3
.
Plugging
\bm{\Xi}_{4111}
in the integral
\bm{\Xi}_{411}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{4111}(r)\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-1}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\sum_{j=\ell+1}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-1}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{2H}\sum_{j=1}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-1}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta),

where the second-to-last inequality follows from Lemma
4.3
and the last one from
Lemma
4.8
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
5.6
.

For any
r\in[0,t_{i})
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{r}^{t}K(\kappa_{s}+\Delta,s)\sqrt{\Delta}K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}\wedge t}K(t_{j+1},s)\sqrt{\Delta}K(t_{j},r)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{1+H}\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i}K(t_{j},r).

Integrating the last two terms of the sum yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}K(t_{i-1},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{t}K(t_{i},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim t_{i}^{2H}.

Since
K(\kappa_{s},r)=0
for
r\in[t_{i},t)
and
s\in[r,t)
, it holds

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{412}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i-2}K(t_{j},r)\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r+\Delta^{H+1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-1}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\sum_{j=\tau(r)}^{i-2}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r+\Delta^{H+1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-1}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\sum_{j=\ell+1}^{i-2}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r+\Delta^{H+1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\sum_{j=\ell+1}^{i-2}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r+\Delta^{H+1},

because for
r\in[t_{\ell},t_{\ell+1})
,
\tau(r)=\ell+1
and hence the inner sum is empty if
\ell\geq i-2
. We continue by separating the term
j=\ell+1
, invoking Lemma
4.3
and
K(t_{j},r)\leq K(t_{j},t_{\ell+1})
:

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{412}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}\Big\{\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}K(t_{j},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{\ell+1},r)\big(K(t_{i},r)-K(t,r)\big)\mathrm{d}r\Big\}+\Delta^{H+1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Delta^{H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{\ell+1},r)\mathrm{d}r+\Delta^{H+1}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{3H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta^{3H+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta^{H+1}.

On the one hand, we already saw in (
4.3
) that
\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\lesssim 1
. On the other hand, we have, similarly to (

(

Ξ
31

)

.
), that
\sum_{j=\ell+2}^{i-2}(j-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim(i-\ell-3)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\leq(i-\ell-1)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}
and
\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-3}(i-\ell-1)^{2H-1}\lesssim(i-1)^{2H}\leq\Delta^{-2H}
.
Overall this results in
\bm{\Xi}_{412}\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}
.
∎

5.2.2.
Bound for
\Xi_{42}

Proposition 5.7
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Xi}_{42}(r)]\right\rvert\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{2H+1}
.

Proof.

This proof heavily replies on IBP formula as well.
Note that, for
r<t<s
,
\varphi_{r}(t)
is
\mathcal{F}_{r}
-measurable and hence
\mathrm{D}^{B}_{s}\varphi_{r}(t)=\rho^{-1}\mathrm{D}^{W}_{s}\varphi_{r}(t)=0
.
Thus we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Xi}_{42}(r)]\right\rvert

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})\big(K(s,r)-K(s,t)\big)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right)\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}^{B}_{s}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})\big(K(s,r)-K(s,t)\big)\mathrm{d}s\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s\in[t,T]}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}^{B}_{s}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})\right]\right\rvert\int_{t}^{T}\left\lvert K(s,r)-K(s,t)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}s.

We recall that for all
s\in[t,T]
,
\mathrm{D}^{B}_{s}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})
is bounded in
L^{2}(\Omega)
.
Thus, the prefactor multiplying the integral in the equation above is bounded.
By Tonelli’s theorem we conclude

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Xi}_{42}(r)]\right\rvert\lesssim\int_{t}^{T}\int_{r}^{t}(s-q)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}q\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\int_{r}^{t}\int_{t}^{T}(s-q)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{r}^{t}(t-q)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim(t-r)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}.

The claim thus follows from
[
17
, Lemma 2.1]
.
∎

5.2.3.
Bound for
\Xi_{43}

Proposition 5.8
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{43}(r)\right\rvert\left\lvert K(t,r)-K(t_{i},r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Proof.

Since
|\rho|\leq 1
, we omit it in the proof.
We show that
\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{43}(r)\right\rvert\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}\mathbbm{1}_{r\leq t_{i}}
for all
r\in[0,t)
. Together with Lemma
4.6
, this yields the claim.
Clark-Ocone formula yields

\displaystyle\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})=\mathbb{E}[\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})]+\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big(\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})K(\kappa_{r},q)-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})K(t_{i},q)\Big)\mathrm{d}W_{q},

and Malliavin IBP with respect to
W
implies

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{43}(r)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(r)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\right]\right]\mathbb{E}[\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\Big[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Big(\mathrm{D}^{W}_{q}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})+\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}^{W}_{q}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\Big)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big(\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})K(\kappa_{r},q)-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})K(t_{i},q)\Big)\mathrm{d}q\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(r)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\right]\right]\mathbb{E}[\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathrm{D}^{W}_{q}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big(\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big)\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathrm{D}^{W}_{q}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\mathbb{E}_{q}\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\Big)\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}^{W}_{q}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big(\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big)\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}^{W}_{q}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\mathbb{E}_{q}\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\Big)\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{430}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{431}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{432}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{433}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{434}(r).

The proof then follows from the lemmas below
and Lemma
4.6
.
∎

Lemma 5.9
.

\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{430}(r)\right\rvert\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Lemma 5.10
.

\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{431}(r)\right\rvert\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Lemma 5.11
.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{432}(r)\right\rvert\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Lemma 5.12
.

|\bm{\Xi}_{433}(r)|\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Lemma 5.13
.

|\bm{\Xi}_{434}(r)|\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}+(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}
.

Proof of Lemma
5.10
.

By virtue of Lemma
3.7
,

\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi(V_{t_{i}})]\right\rvert\lesssim t_{i}^{2H}-\kappa_{r}^{2H}\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}.

Since the prefactor is bounded, this yields the claim.
∎

Proof of Lemma
5.10
.

We recall that, by Lemma
4.2
we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}_{q}\left[\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\right]\right\rvert\lesssim\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\left\lvert\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{x}\right\rvert\mathrm{d}v.

Noticing that
\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}\varphi_{r}(t)=\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{(2)}(V_{t})]K(t,q)
, the first term then yields

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{431}(r)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{(2)}(V_{t})]\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left\lvert\mathbb{E}_{q}\big[\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\big]\right\rvert\Big]K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{(2)}(V_{t})]\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\Big]\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left\{\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{(2)}(V_{t})]\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left\lvert\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{x}\right\rvert\right]\mathrm{d}v\right\}K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{4311}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{4312}(r).

Since
q\leq\kappa_{r}
,
then

\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\leq\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-\kappa_{r})^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
,
and thus

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{4311}(r)\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q\,(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}.

Now, by Cauchy-Schwarz and Itô isometry, it holds

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{4312}(r)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left|\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{x}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}v\right|K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{2H-3}\mathrm{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}vK(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(v-q)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-\kappa_{r})^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}v\right)(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-\frac{1}{2}}K(t,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}K(t,q)(\kappa_{r}-q)^{H-1}\mathrm{d}q\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H},

where we used
K(t,q)\leq K(t_{i},\kappa_{r})
in the last inequality.
∎

Proof of Lemma
5.11
.

Exploiting Lemma
4.5
with
\alpha=H-\frac{1}{2}
, we write

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{432}(r)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{(2)}(V_{t})]\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\mathbb{E}_{q}\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big]\right\rvert K(t,q)\left\lvert K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},q)\left\lvert K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}q\leq(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}.

∎

Proof of Proposition
5.12
.

Let us now move on to discuss

(5.18)

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{433}(r)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big[\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big]\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\mathrm{D}_{q}^{W}X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big[\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big]\mathrm{d}q\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\Bigg\{\int_{\kappa_{q}+\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(\kappa_{s})K(\kappa_{s},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}

\displaystyle\qquad+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\Bigg\}K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathbb{E}_{q}\Big[\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})-\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\Big]\mathrm{d}q\Bigg].

Let
\mathcal{G}_{q}:=\sup_{v\in\mathbb{T},n=2,3}\mathbb{E}_{q}[\psi^{(n)}(V_{v})]
, by Lemmas
4.1
and
4.2
we can write,
with

\bm{W}_{q,v}:=\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{x}
and
\phi^{(4)}_{t}:=\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{433}(r)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left[\int_{\kappa_{q}+\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{s}}})K({\kappa_{s}+\Delta},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right]\mathcal{G}_{q}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\left\lvert\bm{W}_{q,v}\right\rvert\ \mathrm{d}v\Bigg]K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left[\int_{\kappa_{q}+\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{s}}})K({\kappa_{s}+\Delta},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right]\mathcal{G}_{q}\Bigg]\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right]\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{q}\left[\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\right](H-\frac{1}{2})\bm{W}_{q,v}\mathrm{d}v\bigg]K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right]\mathcal{G}_{q}\right]\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\Big[\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left[\int_{\kappa_{q}+\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{s}}})K({\kappa_{s}+\Delta},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right]\mathcal{G}_{q}\right\rvert^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{W}_{q,v}^{2}\right]\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}vK(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}K({\kappa_{q}+\Delta},q)\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right]\mathbb{E}_{q}[\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\bm{W}_{q,v}\right]\right\rvert\mathrm{d}vK(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{4331}+\bm{\Xi}_{4332}+\bm{\Xi}_{4333}+\bm{\Xi}_{4334}.

The rest of the proof follows from the lemmas below.
∎

We shall need the following technical lemma repeatedly, where we introduce

(5.19)

\mathfrak{P}_{\alpha}(\kappa_{r},t_{i}):=\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q.

Lemma 5.14
.

For
\alpha+H+1\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}_{\alpha}(\kappa_{r},t_{i})\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1}\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}
.

Lemma 5.15
.

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{4331}|\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Lemma 5.16
.

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{4332}|\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{3H}\Delta^{H}
.

Lemma 5.17
.

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{4333}|\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Lemma 5.18
.

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{4334}|\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
.

Proof of Lemma
5.14
.

In the following, we use
(v-q)^{\alpha}=(v-q)^{-H}(v-q)^{\alpha+H}\leq(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-H}(v-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H}
, for all
q\leq\kappa_{r}\leq v
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}vK(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-H}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1}\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-H}K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1}\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-\frac{1}{2}}((\kappa_{q}+\Delta)-q)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1}\left\{\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(t_{j+1}-q)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}q+\int_{\kappa_{r}-\Delta}^{\kappa_{r}}(\kappa_{r}-q)^{H-1}\mathrm{d}q\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1}\left\{\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}(\kappa_{r}-t_{j+1})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}(t_{j+1}-q)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}q+\Delta^{H}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1}\left\{\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}(\tau(r)-1-({j+1}))^{-\frac{1}{2}}+1\right\}\lesssim\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{\alpha+H+1},

where we estimated the sum in the following way with
k=\tau(r)-1
:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{k-2}({k}-({j+1}))^{-\frac{1}{2}}+1=\sum_{j=1}^{k-1}j^{-\frac{1}{2}}+1

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{k-1}s^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s+1

\displaystyle=\frac{(k-1)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2}+1=\frac{\sqrt{t_{k-1}}+\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.15
.

A similar argument as for Lemma
4.1
entails

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{\kappa_{q}+\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{s}}})K({\kappa_{s}+\Delta},q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\right)\mathcal{G}_{q}\right\rvert^{2}\right]\lesssim\left(1+K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta}\right)^{2},

while Itô isometry and routine integration yield

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{x}\right\rvert^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}=\Bigg(\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{2H-3}\mathrm{d}x\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg((v-q)^{2H-2}-v^{2H-2}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(v-q)^{H-1}.

Therefore, thanks to Lemma
5.14
, we conclude

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{4331}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)\left(1+K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta}\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}(v-q)^{-H}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q+\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{H-1}(\kappa_{r},t_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-\kappa_{r})^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)(\kappa_{r}-q)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}q+\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{H-1}(\kappa_{r},t_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}+\Delta^{H}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.16
.

Since
\bm{\Xi}_{4332}=\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{2H-1}(\kappa_{r},t_{i})
,
with
\mathfrak{P}_{2H-1}
introduced in (
5.19
),
the result follows from Lemma
5.14
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
5.17
.

For the term
\bm{\Xi}_{4333}
we need sharp bounds, hence we applying Malliavin integration by parts, which generates several new terms
(here
\phi_{t}:=\phi(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})
and likewise for its derivatives):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{x}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{D}_{x}^{W}\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right](v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\mathrm{D}_{x}^{W}\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right](v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\mathrm{D}_{x}^{W}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right](v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathrm{D}_{x}^{W}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right](v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

(5.20)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t})]\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right]K(t,x)(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

(5.21)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(5)}_{t}\left\{\int_{x}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},x)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,x)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{x}})\right\}\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\bigg](v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

(5.22)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})K(s,x)K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})K(\kappa_{q},x)\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right](v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

(5.23)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\left\{\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\right]K(t_{i},x)(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{q}K(t_{i},x)(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{0}^{q}\left\{(1+K(\kappa_{x}+\Delta,x)\sqrt{\Delta}\right\}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{0}^{q}K(\kappa_{q},x)(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{43331}(v,q)+\bm{\Xi}_{43332}(v,q)+\bm{\Xi}_{43333}(v,q).

In the last step we leveraged the fact that the expectations in (
5.20
) and (
5.23
) are bounded uniformly, hence both these terms contribute to
\bm{\Xi}_{43331}
.
The estimate for line (
5.21
) is a consequence of Lemma
4.1
and it contributes to
\bm{\Xi}_{43332}
.
Then we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to estimate (
5.22
) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}_{t}\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})K(s,x)K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{s}\mathbb{E}_{q}\left[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{t_{i}})\right]\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\int_{t}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})^{2}]K(s,x)^{2}K(s,q)^{2}\mathrm{d}s\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\int_{t}^{T}K(s,q)^{2}\mathrm{d}s\,\sup_{s\in\mathbb{T}}\mathbb{E}[\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})^{2}]K(t,x)^{2}\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim K(t,x)\leq K(t_{i},x).

Thus, this term contributes to
\bm{\Xi}_{43333}
.
Note that, for all
q<\kappa_{r}<v
, then

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{43331}(v,q)\leq\int_{0}^{q}(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}x\,K(t_{i},q)\lesssim(v-q)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}K(t_{i},\kappa_{r})\leq K(\kappa_{r},q)K(t_{i},\kappa_{r}).

Plugging this back into the double integral we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\bm{\Xi}_{43331}(v,q)\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q\lesssim\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(t_{i},\kappa_{r})\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}K(\kappa_{r},q)^{2}\mathrm{d}q\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}.

For the term
\bm{\Xi}_{43332}
we investigate

(5.24)

\int_{0}^{q}K(\kappa_{x}+\Delta,x)\mathrm{d}x=\sum_{j=1}^{\tau(q)-2}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}K(t_{j},x)\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\kappa_{q}}^{q}K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,x)\mathrm{d}x\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{\tau(q)-1}\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\leq\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}.

Once we insert this back in the double integral, since
1\lesssim K(t_{i},x)
, it entails

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\bm{\Xi}_{43332}(v,q)\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\bm{\Xi}_{43331}(v,q)\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{0}^{q}K(\kappa_{x}+\Delta,x)\mathrm{d}x\right)(v-q)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}vK(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{H}\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\Big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\Big)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{H}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H},

where we leveraged Lemma
4.5
with
\alpha=H-\frac{1}{2}
to obtain the last inequality.
Finally, we exploit
(v-x)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\leq(v-q)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}
with
x\leq q
in the last term to reach, thanks to Lemma
4.5
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}\bm{\Xi}_{43333}(v,q)\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{0}^{q}K(\kappa_{q},x)\mathrm{d}x\right)\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\Big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\Big)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.18
.

Since

\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\leq\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-\kappa_{r})^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}
,

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{4334}=\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(v-q)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}v\right)K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}\int_{0}^{\kappa_{r}}K(\kappa_{r},q)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.13
.

By Lemma
4.1
we get the following decomposition:

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{434}(r)|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\varphi_{r}(t)\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\Bigg\{\int_{q}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},q)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,q)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\Bigg\}\mathbb{E}_{q}[\psi^{\prime}(V_{t_{i}})]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\Big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\Big)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Big(\sqrt{\Delta}K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)+1\Big)\Big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\Big)\mathrm{d}q=:\bm{\Xi}_{4341}(r)+\bm{\Xi}_{4342}(r).

For
j\leq\tau(r)-2
, let us define

(5.25)

\vartheta_{j}:=\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t_{j+1},q)\big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\big)\mathrm{d}q.

Recall that
\kappa_{r}=t_{\tau(r)-1}
. Thus, we have

(5.26)

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{4341}(r)|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t_{j+1},q)\big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\big)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\int_{\kappa_{r}-\Delta}^{\kappa_{r}}K(\kappa_{r},q)\big(K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\big)\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{j=\tau(r)-1}^{i-2}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t_{j+1},q)K(t_{i},q)\mathrm{d}q+\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},q)^{2}\mathrm{d}q\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}\vartheta_{j}+\int_{\kappa_{r}-\Delta}^{\kappa_{r}}K(\kappa_{r},q)^{2}\mathrm{d}q

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\sum_{j=\tau(r)-1}^{i-2}K(t_{i},t_{j+1})\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t_{j+1},q)\mathrm{d}q+\Delta^{2H}\Bigg\}

(5.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}\vartheta_{j}+\Delta^{2H}+\sum_{j=\tau(r)-1}^{i-2}K(t_{i},t_{j+1})\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{2H}\Bigg\}

We note that, for
q\in[t_{j},t_{j+1})
and
t_{j+1}<\kappa_{r}
,

\displaystyle K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(s-q)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}s\leq(\kappa_{r}-t_{j+1})^{-H-1}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{t_{i}}(s-\kappa_{r})^{2H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\Delta^{-H-1}(\tau(r)-j-2)^{-H-1}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H+\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore, from (
5.25
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Delta}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}\vartheta_{j}\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau(r)-3}(\tau(r)-j-2)^{-H-1}\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H+\frac{1}{2}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\tau(r)-1}^{i-2}K(t_{i},t_{j+1})\lesssim\sum_{j=\tau(r)-1}^{i-2}(i-(j+1))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H-\frac{1}{2}}(i-\tau(r)-1)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\Delta^{-1}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}},

where we exploited
i-\tau(r)-1=\Delta^{-1}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})
.
Overall, from (
5.27
), we get

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{4341}(r)\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{2H+\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{H}(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{2H+\frac{1}{2}}.

Furthermore,

\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{4342}(r)\right\rvert\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left\lvert K(\kappa_{r},q)-K(t_{i},q)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}q\lesssim(t_{i}-\kappa_{r})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}
, by Lemma
4.4

and the claim follows.
∎

5.3.
Bound for
\Xi_{5}

Recall that
\mathcal{G}_{r}=\sup_{s\in\mathbb{T},n=2,3}\mathbb{E}_{r}[\psi^{(n)}(V_{s})]
and define

\displaystyle Z(t,r):=\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{s})K(s,r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right),

where we interpret the first integral to be null if
\kappa_{r}+2\Delta\geq t
, i.e.
r\geq t_{i-1}
.
Note that
Z(t,r)
is uniformly bounded in
L^{p}
for all
p>1
, by virtue of the growth of
\phi,\psi
and their derivatives, and the proof of Lemma
4.1
. Equation (
5.8
) shows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{r}

\displaystyle=\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\int_{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}^{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{s}})K(\kappa_{s},r)\mathrm{d}B_{s}+Z(t,r)

\displaystyle=\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})(B_{{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}}-B_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})K({\kappa_{r}+\Delta},r)+Z(t,r).

Exploiting this and Lemma
4.2
yields,
writing and
\bm{W}_{r,s}:=\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{u}
,

(5.29)

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{5}=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathcal{U}_{r}\big(\varphi_{r}(t)-\varphi_{r}(t_{i})\big)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{t}\Bigg[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})(B_{{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}}-B_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})\Bigg]K({\kappa_{r}+\Delta},r)+Z(t,r)\Bigg)\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\Bigg(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})]\bm{W}_{r,s}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{(3)}(V_{s})](s-r)^{2H-1}\right)\mathrm{d}s\Bigg)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})(B_{{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}}-B_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})\mathcal{G}_{r}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left|\bm{W}_{r,s}\right|\mathrm{d}s\Bigg]K({\kappa_{r}+\Delta},r)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\psi^{\prime}(V_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})\mathcal{G}_{r}(B_{{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}}-B_{{\kappa_{r}+\Delta}})\Bigg]K({\kappa_{r}+\Delta},r)\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}sK(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[Z(t,r)\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})]\bm{W}_{r,s}\mathrm{d}s\right]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\Big[Z(t,r)\mathcal{G}_{r}\Big]\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r=:\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bm{\Xi}_{5i},

where we used (
4.3
).
We shall need the following lemma, mimicking Lemma
5.14
, but with slightly different arguments in with respect to the ones used in (
5.19
).

Lemma 5.19
.

For
\alpha\in(-1,0)
and
t\in[t_{i},t_{i+1})
,

\mathfrak{P}_{\alpha}(t_{i},t)\lesssim\Delta^{2H+\alpha+1}\left(\Delta^{-\alpha-H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H>-\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H<-\frac{1}{2}}+|\log(\Delta)|\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H=-\frac{1}{2}}+1\right).

Proof.

Since

\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(t_{i}-t_{j+1})^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim(i-j-1)^{\alpha}\Delta^{1+\alpha}
,

\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t_{j+1},r)\mathrm{d}r=\frac{(t_{j+1}-t_{j})^{H+\frac{1}{2}}}{H+\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}

and

\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-t_{i})^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}s=\frac{1}{1+\alpha}(t-t_{i})^{1+\alpha}\lesssim\Delta^{1+\alpha}
,
then we obtain

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}_{\alpha}(t_{i},t)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(\kappa_{r}+\Delta,r)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-3}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(t_{i}-t_{j+1})^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{j+1},r)(t_{i}-t_{j+1})^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{t_{i-2}}^{t_{i-1}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-t_{i})^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i-1},r)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r+\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-t_{i})^{\alpha}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)^{2}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{2H+\alpha+1}\sum_{j=0}^{i-3}(i-j-1)^{\alpha+H-\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta^{1+\alpha}\left(\int_{t_{i-2}}^{t_{i-1}}K(t_{i-1},r)^{2}\mathrm{d}r+\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)^{2}\mathrm{d}r\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{2H+\alpha+1}\left(\Delta^{-\alpha-H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H>-\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H<-\frac{1}{2}}-\log(\Delta)\mathbbm{1}_{\alpha+H=-\frac{1}{2}}\right)+\Delta^{2H+\alpha+1}.

Indeed,
since
\alpha+H-\frac{1}{2}<0
by assumption, then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{i-3}(i-j-1)^{\alpha+H-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{i-2}(i-s-1)^{\alpha+H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle\frac{(i-1)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}-1}{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Delta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\alpha-H},&\text{if }\alpha+H>-\frac{1}{2},\\
\displaystyle\frac{(i-1)^{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}-1}{\alpha+H+\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 1,&\text{if }\alpha+H<-\frac{1}{2},\\
\displaystyle\log(i-1)\sim|\log(\Delta)|,&\text{if }\alpha+H=-\frac{1}{2}.\end{array}\right.

∎

Proposition 5.20
.

\displaystyle|\bm{\Xi}_{5}|\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Proof.

Since

|\bm{\Xi}_{5}|\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bm{\Xi}_{5i}
,
the result follows from Lemmas
5.21
-
5.22
-
5.23
-
5.24
.
∎

Lemma 5.21
.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{51}\right\rvert\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{H-1}(t_{i},t)\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Lemma 5.22
.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{52}\right\rvert\lesssim\Delta^{4H+\frac{1}{2}}\left(\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}-3H}\mathbbm{1}_{H>\frac{1}{6}}+\mathbbm{1}_{H<\frac{1}{6}}+|\log(\Delta)|\mathbbm{1}_{H=\frac{1}{6}}+1\right)\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta).

Lemma 5.23
.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{53}\right\rvert\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Lemma 5.24
.

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{54}\right\rvert\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Proof of Lemma
5.21
.

By Cauchy-Schwarz and Itô isometry, we write

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{51}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left\{\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{2H-3}\mathrm{d}u\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(\kappa_{r}+\Delta,r)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{H-1}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(\kappa_{r}+\Delta,r)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r=\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{H-1}(t_{i},t),

and the proof follows from Lemma
5.19
since

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{H-1}(t_{i},t)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{3H+\frac{1}{2}}\left[\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}-2H}\mathbbm{1}_{H>\frac{1}{4}}+\mathbbm{1}_{H<\frac{1}{4}}+|\log(\Delta)|\mathbbm{1}_{H=\frac{1}{4}}+1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{(3H+\frac{1}{2})\land 1}\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta).

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.22
.

As in the
\bm{\Xi}_{51}
case, Cauchy-Schwarz and Lemma
5.19
yield

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{52}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}sK(\kappa_{r}+\Delta,r)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r=\sqrt{\Delta}\mathfrak{P}_{2H-1}(t_{i},t)\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta).

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.24
.

It is immediate from
Lemma
4.4
since

\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{54}\right\rvert\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left((t_{i}-r)^{2H}-(t-r)^{2H}\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\diamondsuit_{2H}(\Delta)=\bigstar(\Delta).

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.23
.

By IBP with respect to
W
, the inner integrand decomposes as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[Z(t,r)\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})]\left(\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}W_{u}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{r}\Big(\mathrm{D}^{W}_{u}Z(t,r)\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})]+Z(t,r)\mathrm{D}^{W}_{u}\mathbb{E}_{r}[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})]\Big)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{D}^{W}_{u}\phi^{(3)}_{t}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}^{t}})K(\kappa_{q}^{t},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\right](s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{(3)}_{t}\mathrm{D}^{W}_{u}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}^{t}})K(\kappa_{q}^{t},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\right](s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{(3)}_{t}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}^{t}})K(\kappa_{q}^{t},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)\mathrm{D}^{W}_{u}(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\right](s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{531}(s,r)+\bm{\Xi}_{532}(s,r)+\bm{\Xi}_{533}(s,r),

again writing
\phi^{(3)}_{t}:=\phi^{(3)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})
,
and the proof follows from the three lemmas below.
∎

Lemma 5.25
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{531}(s,r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{1+H}
.

Lemma 5.26
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{532}(s,r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Lemma 5.27
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{533}(s,r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta)
.

Remark 5.28
.

Some terms in the proofs below are reminiscent of the integrals in
\bm{\Xi}_{4333}
. We however here aim for a lower error (
\bigstar(\Delta)
vs
\Delta^{2H})
, which requires sharper estimates.

The proofs are given in order of increasing length.

Proof of Lemma
5.27
.

Since
Z(t,r)\in L^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{533}(s,r)\right\rvert

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\int_{0}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[Z(t,r)(\psi^{2})^{(3)}(V_{s})\right]K(s,u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\right\rvert

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{2H-2}\mathrm{d}u\lesssim(s-r)^{2H-1},

and with that we conclude,
exploiting Lemma
4.4
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left\{\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{533}(s,r)\right\rvert\mathrm{d}s\right\}K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Big[(t-r)^{2H}-(t_{i}-r)^{2H}\Big]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\bigstar(\Delta).

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.25
.

By virtue of Lemma
4.1
, we estimate

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{\Xi}_{531}\right\rvert(s,r)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{r}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\phi^{(4)}(X_{T}^{t,\overline{X}_{t}})\left(\int_{u}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa^{t}_{q}})K(\kappa^{t}_{q},u)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}^{t}})K(\kappa_{q}^{t},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\rho\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\bigg](s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

(5.30)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{r}\Big(1+\sqrt{\Delta}K(\kappa_{u}+\Delta,u)\Big)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

(5.31)

\displaystyle\lesssim(s-r)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{\Delta}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\tau(r)-2}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{\ell+1},u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

(5.32)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{r}K(\kappa_{r}+\Delta,u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

(5.33)

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{5311}(s,r)+\bm{\Xi}_{5312}(s,r)+\bm{\Xi}_{5313}(s,r).

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{5311})}

To begin with, we have, by Lemma
4.4
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{5311}(s,r)\mathrm{d}sK(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}sK(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Big[(t_{i}-r)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}-(t-r)^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\Big]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\diamondsuit_{H+\frac{1}{2}}(\Delta)=\Delta.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{5312})}

We have, for
\ell\leq\tau(r)-2
and
s\in[t_{i},t]
,

\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{\ell+1},u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\leq(t_{i}-t_{\ell+1})^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{t_{\ell}}^{t_{\ell+1}}K(t_{\ell+1},u)\mathrm{d}u\leq\Delta^{2H-1}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}.

We first deal with the interval
[t_{i-1},t_{i}]
, and exploiting the previous lines, we have

\displaystyle\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{5312}(s,r)\mathrm{d}s\right]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\Delta}\Delta^{2H-1}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{\tau(r)-2}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\right]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{2H+\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{i-2}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\right]\lesssim\Delta^{3H+1},

since the sum is bounded, as it can be seen from (
4.3
) for
\alpha=0
.
We observe that, on
[0,t_{i-1}]
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}\left[\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{5312}(s,r)\mathrm{d}s\right]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\leq\sqrt{\Delta}\Delta^{2H-1}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\mathrm{d}s\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{\tau(r)-2}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\right]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r.

The sum is estimated as the one above, albeit with the advantage that
\tau(r)\leq i-1
:

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{\tau(r)-2}(i-\ell-1)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\lesssim(i-\tau(r))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-(i-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}=\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}-H}\Big[(t_{i-1}-\kappa(r))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-t_{i-1}^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\Big],

which entails

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{5312}(s,r)\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{1+H}\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}\Big[(t_{i-1}-\kappa(r))^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-t_{i-1}^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\Big]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{1+H}\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}(t_{i-1}-r)^{2H-1}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\Delta^{1+H}.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{5313})}
For the last term

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{5313}(s,r)\mathrm{d}qK(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\sqrt{\Delta}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\int_{t_{i}}^{t}(s-r)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}s\int_{\kappa_{r}}^{r}K(r,u)\mathrm{d}uK(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Big[(t_{i}-r)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}-(t-r)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\Big]K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+1}\diamondsuit_{H-\frac{1}{2}}(\Delta)=\Delta^{3H+1}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
5.26
.

We compute

\displaystyle\mathrm{D}_{u}^{W}\left(\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}^{t}})K(\kappa_{q}^{t},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\psi(V_{\kappa_{r}})\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})K(\kappa_{q},u)K(\kappa_{q},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{q})K(q,u)K(q,r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}+\rho\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{u}})K(\kappa_{u},r)+\rho\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})K(\kappa_{r},u),

where
K(\kappa_{u},r)=0
since
u\leq r
.
By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Itô isometry,

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{532}(s,r)

\displaystyle\lesssim\overbrace{\int_{0}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{t}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})K(\kappa_{q},u)K(\kappa_{q},r)\mathrm{d}B_{q}\right](s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u}^{{=:\bm{\Xi}_{5321}(s,r)}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{r}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{q})^{2}K(q,u)^{2}K(q,r)^{2}\mathrm{d}q\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{r}})^{2}K(\kappa_{r},u)^{2}\right]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Xi}_{5321}(s,r)+\int_{0}^{r}\bigg\{\int_{t}^{T}K(t,u)^{2}K(q,t)^{2}\mathrm{d}q+K(\kappa_{r},u)^{2}\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Xi}_{5321}(s,r)+\int_{0}^{r}K(t,u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u+\int_{0}^{r}K(\kappa_{r},u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=:\bm{\Xi}_{5321}(s,r)+\bm{\Xi}_{5322}(s,r)+\bm{\Xi}_{5323}(s,r).

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{5322})}
.
Since
u\leq r\leq s\leq t
, then
K(t,u)\leq K(s,r)
and
\int_{0}^{r}(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\leq K(s,r)
.
Thus

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\Big(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\int_{0}^{r}

\displaystyle K(t,u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}K(s,r)^{2}\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\big((t-r)^{2H}-(t_{i}-r)^{2H}\big)\mathrm{d}r=\diamondsuit_{2H}(\Delta)=\bigstar(\Delta),

where in the last step we have exploited Lemma
4.4
for
\alpha=2H
.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{5323})}
.
For all
j=0,\cdots,i-1
, let

(5.34)

\xi_{j}:=\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\int_{0}^{t_{j}}K(t_{j},u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s.

We write

(5.35)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{i}}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\bm{\Xi}_{5323}(s,r)\mathrm{d}s\right)K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r

(5.36)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left(\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\right)\int_{t_{i}}^{t}\int_{0}^{t_{j}}K(t_{j},u)(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{i-2}\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{j}+\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K(t_{i},r)\mathrm{d}r\,\xi_{i-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{i-2}(i-j-1)^{H-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{j}+\Delta^{H+\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{i-1}.

\mathbf{(\bm{\Xi}_{5321})}
.
Another application of the IBP formula to
\bm{\Xi}_{5321}(s,r)
yields

(5.37)

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{5321}(s,r)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{r}\int_{\kappa_{r}+2\Delta}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{D}_{q}^{B}\left(\phi^{(3)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\right)\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right]K(\kappa_{q},u)K(\kappa_{q},r)\mathrm{d}q\,(s-u)^{H-\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{d}u,

where we compute, applying Lemma
4.1
to the first term and noticing that the other expectations are uniformly bounded,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathrm{D}_{q}^{B}

\displaystyle\left(\phi^{(3)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\right)\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\rho\mathbb{E}\left[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\phi^{(4)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})\int_{q}^{t}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\kappa_{\ell}})K(\kappa_{\ell},q)\mathrm{d}B_{\ell}\right]

\displaystyle+\rho\mathbb{E}\left[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\phi^{(4)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})\int_{t}^{T}\psi^{\prime}(V_{\ell})K(\ell,q)\mathrm{d}B_{\ell}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[(\psi^{2})^{\prime\prime}(V_{s})\phi^{(4)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})\psi(V_{\kappa_{q}})\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right]+\rho\mathbb{E}\left[\phi^{(3)}(X^{t,\overline{X}_{t}}_{T})(\psi^{2})^{(3)}(V_{s})\psi^{\prime\prime}(V_{\kappa_{q}})\right]K(s,q)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+K(\kappa_{q}+\Delta,q)\sqrt{\Delta})\mathbbm{1}_{q<t_{i}}+1+K(s,q).

This implies, since
1\lesssim K(s,q)
,
